    WHEN 1 THEN N'OrangeMoney'
    WHEN 2 THEN N'MtnMoney'
    WHEN 3 THEN N'Especes'
    WHEN 4 THEN N'M2u'
    WHEN 5 THEN N'SaraMoney'
    ELSE CAST([p].[ModePaiement] AS nvarchar(max))
END AS [ModePaiement], [b0].[Reference] AS [BienReference]
FROM [Paiements] AS [p]
